out[-1] = p\n+                    continue\n+            out.append(p)\n+        return out\n+\n+\n+def closest_route_point(route, pos):\n+    if not route or len(route) == 1:\n+        return ll = dx*dx + dy*dy\n+        f = 0 if ll == 0 else max(0.0, min(1.0, ((pos[0]-a[0])*dx+(pos[1]-a[1])*dy)/ll))\n+        p = (a[0]+f*dx, a[1]+f*dy)\n+        d = distance(pos, p)\n+        if d < route[-1] if route else pos\n+    _, idx = closest_route_point(route, pos)\n+    # Find the projection and walk forward along the route by `amount`.\n+    a, b = route[idx], route[idx+1]\n+    dx, dy
